# Backend Features Progress Tracker

This document tracks the implementation progress of the reconciled backend features for the `invoice-be` component, aligned with the frontend screen requirements. Feature spec files (`feature_1..11_*.md`) describe the target design only — every open item, bug, and pending build task is tracked here instead, so status doesn't drift out of sync across files.

**Current Status:** ~91% complete against [Technical_Architecture_Document.md](../../../docs/architecture/Technical_Architecture_Document.md), with 11 open items below. *(Recalculated Jul 24, 2026 — merged two parallel closure sets: Gaps 3, 4, 41, 42, 43, 44 and Feature 3.1 (extraction pipeline), plus Gaps 1b, 5, 6, 8, and 29 (Feature 10 AI Trainer backend redesign) — see the respective sections below. Updated Jul 25, 2026 — live end-to-end UI testing of that merge (real Azure OpenAI/Doc Intelligence, real browser sessions, not just unit tests) found and closed 6 more issues the merge introduced or left unverified: Gaps 46-51, plus new Gap 48 wiring committed Trainer rules into the Chat agent for the first time. Updated Jul 31, 2026 — Feature 11 (PayU Billing) built; Clerk-list Gap 4 (auth enforcement) reconciled from `auth-feature-4`, both marked in-progress/partial below; new Gap 71 flags that billing lapse enforcement is currently a dead code path. Updated Aug 2, 2026 — **Feature 1.1 (Granular RBAC) built** (`[~]`, code-complete/deployment-unverified), which also **resolved Clerk-list Gap 4's B1 blocker** (invoice-fe now sends a real Authorization header) and ran that gap's long-deferred full-suite regression: 184 passed / 1 pre-existing environmental failure / 5 deselected. Also Aug 2, 2026 — **Feature 11's PayU return flow completed**: the `/billing/success` + `/billing/failed` 404 closed, `surl`/`furl` made publicly reachable via an invoice-website pass-through, `FRONTEND_URL`/`PUBLIC_APP_URL` disambiguated, and `tests/test_billing.py` added — suite now 204 passed / same 1 pre-existing failure / 5 deselected.)*
